import 'package:dio/dio.dart';
import 'clock.dart';
import 'config.dart';
import 'context.dart';
import 'key.dart';
import 'options.dart';
import 'pipeline.dart';
import 'plugin.dart';
import 'request.dart';
import 'response.dart';
import 'stages.dart';
import 'transport.dart';

/// 关闭 debug 时复用的空日志器。
void _noLog(String _) {}

/// 以 Dio 为引擎的洋葱管线客户端；旧入口与本库完全独立。
class Dioman<R extends DiomanResponse<dynamic>> {
  /// 创建客户端，例如 `Dioman(dio: Dio(), debug: true)`。
  /// [response] 控制归一化，[reject] 只转换动词方法的可预期失败。
  Dioman(
      {required this.dio,
      List<DiomanPlugin<R>> plugins = const [],
      DiomanResponseFactory<R>? response,
      this.key = const DiomanKeyOptions(),
      this.reject,
      Object? debug,
      this.clock = const DiomanClock.system()})
      : response = response ?? DiomanResponseFactory<R>(),
        _debug = debug != null && debug != false,
        _logger =
            debug is DiomanLogger ? debug : (debug == true ? print : _noLog) {
    if (debug != null && debug is! bool && debug is! DiomanLogger) {
      throw ArgumentError.value(debug, 'debug');
    }
    validateDiomanPlugins(plugins);
    try {
      for (final plugin in plugins) {
        _registrations.add(DiomanRegistration(
            plugin, plugin.enabled ? plugin.setup(this) : null));
      }
      _compile();
    } catch (_) {
      for (final registration in _registrations.reversed) {
        try {
          registration.instance?.dispose();
        } catch (_) {/* 保留安装时首次异常。 */}
      }
      rethrow;
    }
  }

  /// 底层 Dio；用户拦截器在每次传输时运行。
  final Dio dio;

  /// 客户端的归一化服务。
  final DiomanResponseFactory<R> response;

  /// 客户端构键配置。
  final DiomanKeyOptions key;

  /// 可预期失败的最终异常转换器。
  final Object Function(R)? reject;

  /// 可注入时间服务。
  final DiomanClock clock;

  /// 是否记录调用点。
  final bool _debug;

  /// 用户日志器或默认输出。
  final DiomanLogger _logger;

  /// 注册顺序，供生命周期和 extend 使用。
  final _registrations = <DiomanRegistration<R>>[];

  /// 已排序的执行层快照。
  List<DiomanRegistration<R>> _layers = [];

  /// 编译后的链，在途请求捕获当前值。
  late DiomanRunner<R> _run;

  /// 活跃逻辑请求。
  final _pending = <DiomanContextState>{};

  /// 请求序号。
  int _sequence = 0;

  /// 生命周期状态。
  bool _disposed = false;

  /// 当前活跃逻辑请求数。
  int get pending => _pending.length;

  /// 活跃的内核取消等待订阅总数，供不变量检查。
  int get cancellationSubscriptions =>
      _pending.fold(0, (n, s) => n + s.listeners.length);

  /// 隔离日志回调异常，避免 debug 改变请求结果。
  void _log(String message) {
    try {
      _logger(message);
    } catch (_) {/* 日志通知不影响请求。 */}
  }

  /// 记录插件通知故障；[message] 交给用户日志器或默认输出，日志器异常会隔离。
  /// 例如插件捕获回调错误后调用 `client.logError('通知失败')`，不受 debug 开关抑制。
  void logError(String message) {
    if (_debug) {
      _log(message);
    } else {
      try {
        print(message);
      } catch (_) {/* 输出通知不影响请求。 */}
    }
  }

  /// 拒绝销毁后的操作。
  void _checkAlive() {
    if (_disposed) throw StateError('客户端已销毁');
  }

  /// 在配置变动时编译链，不在请求期间排序。
  void _compile() {
    _layers = sortDiomanPipeline(_registrations);
    _run = compileDiomanPipeline(_layers, (ctx) => diomanTransport(dio, ctx));
  }

  /// 添加插件；先校验再 setup，安装失败不修改当前链。
  void use(DiomanPlugin<R> plugin) {
    _checkAlive();
    validateDiomanPlugins([..._registrations.map((r) => r.plugin), plugin]);
    final instance = plugin.enabled ? plugin.setup(this) : null;
    _registrations.add(DiomanRegistration(plugin, instance));
    _compile();
  }

  /// 按 [name] 移除并释放插件；不存在返回 false，在途保留原链。
  bool eject(String name) {
    final index = _registrations.indexWhere((r) => r.plugin.name == name);
    if (index < 0) return false;
    final removed = _registrations.removeAt(index);
    _compile();
    removed.instance?.dispose();
    return true;
  }

  /// 返回实现 [A] 的第一个实例，例如 `api.plugin<CacheApi>()`。
  A? plugin<A>() {
    for (final r in _registrations) {
      if (r.instance is A) return r.instance as A;
    }
    return null;
  }

  /// 返回解析后的固定阶段和名称顺序，返回列表不可修改。
  List<(DiomanStage, String)> inspect() => List.unmodifiable(_layers.map((r) {
        final fixed = diomanBuiltinStages.entries
            .where((e) => e.value.contains(r.plugin.name));
        return (
          fixed.isEmpty ? r.plugin.stage : fixed.single.key,
          r.plugin.name
        );
      }));

  /// 中止尚未取消的逻辑请求，返回本次中止数。
  int cancelAll([Object? reason]) {
    var count = 0;
    for (final state in _pending.toList()) {
      if (!state.token.isCancelled) {
        count++;
        state.cancel(reason);
      }
    }
    return count;
  }

  /// 克隆 BaseOptions 并重新 setup 插件，状态不共享；[plugins] 替换继承列表。
  /// [options] 在 setup 后合并非空、非默认字段，显式覆盖优先；容器按键合并。
  /// BaseOptions 不记录省略信息，不能用默认值重置已有非默认值。
  /// 例如 `api.extend(options: BaseOptions(baseUrl: 'https://child.example'))`。
  Dioman<R> extend({BaseOptions? options, List<DiomanPlugin<R>>? plugins}) {
    _checkAlive();
    final child = Dioman<R>(
        dio: Dio(cloneDiomanOptions(dio.options)),
        plugins: plugins ?? _registrations.map((r) => r.plugin).toList(),
        response: response,
        key: key,
        reject: reject,
        debug: _debug ? _logger : false,
        clock: clock);
    if (options != null) mergeDiomanOptions(child.dio.options, options);
    return child;
  }

  /// 先取消请求，再逆序释放全部实例；清理完毕后原栈抛出首个异常。
  void dispose() {
    if (_disposed) return;
    _disposed = true;
    cancelAll();
    final errors = <(Object, StackTrace)>[];
    for (final r in _registrations.reversed) {
      try {
        r.instance?.dispose();
      } catch (e, s) {
        errors.add((e, s));
      }
    }
    if (errors.isNotEmpty) {
      Error.throwWithStackTrace(errors.first.$1, errors.first.$2);
    }
  }

  /// 执行请求并返回结果；可预期失败不抛，internal 原异常原栈抛出。
  /// 例如 `await api.send<Object?>('GET', '/pets')`。
  Future<DiomanResponse<T>> send<T>(String method, String path,
      {Map<String, dynamic>? query, Object? data, DiomanCall? call}) async {
    final result =
        await _dispatch(method, path, query: query, data: data, call: call);
    if (result is DiomanResponse<T>) return result;
    return result.copy<T>();
  }

  /// 执行任意 [method] 并返回数据；可预期失败经 reject 后抛出。
  Future<T> request<T>(String method, String path,
      {Map<String, dynamic>? query, Object? data, DiomanCall? call}) async {
    final result =
        await _dispatch(method, path, query: query, data: data, call: call);
    if (!result.ok) throw (reject?.call(result as R) ?? result);
    return result.data as T;
  }

  /// 发送 GET 并返回数据；参数与 request 相同，例如 `api.get<Object?>('/pets')`。
  Future<T> get<T>(String path,
          {Map<String, dynamic>? query, Object? data, DiomanCall? call}) =>
      request<T>('GET', path, query: query, data: data, call: call);

  /// 发送 POST 并返回数据；参数与 request 相同，例如 `api.post<Object?>('/pets')`。
  Future<T> post<T>(String path,
          {Map<String, dynamic>? query, Object? data, DiomanCall? call}) =>
      request<T>('POST', path, query: query, data: data, call: call);

  /// 发送 PUT 并返回数据；参数与 request 相同，例如 `api.put<Object?>('/pets')`。
  Future<T> put<T>(String path,
          {Map<String, dynamic>? query, Object? data, DiomanCall? call}) =>
      request<T>('PUT', path, query: query, data: data, call: call);

  /// 发送 PATCH 并返回数据；参数与 request 相同，例如 `api.patch<Object?>('/pets')`。
  Future<T> patch<T>(String path,
          {Map<String, dynamic>? query, Object? data, DiomanCall? call}) =>
      request<T>('PATCH', path, query: query, data: data, call: call);

  /// 发送 DELETE 并返回数据；参数与 request 相同，例如 `api.delete<Object?>('/pets')`。
  Future<T> delete<T>(String path,
          {Map<String, dynamic>? query, Object? data, DiomanCall? call}) =>
      request<T>('DELETE', path, query: query, data: data, call: call);

  /// 发送 HEAD 并返回数据；参数与 request 相同，例如 `api.head<Object?>('/pets')`。
  Future<T> head<T>(String path,
          {Map<String, dynamic>? query, Object? data, DiomanCall? call}) =>
      request<T>('HEAD', path, query: query, data: data, call: call);

  /// 入口仅 compose 一次；登记后所有可能抛错的构造都在 finally 保护内。
  Future<DiomanResponse<dynamic>> _dispatch(String method, String path,
      {Map<String, dynamic>? query, Object? data, DiomanCall? call}) async {
    _checkAlive();
    final run = _run;
    final site = _debug ? StackTrace.current : null;
    final state = DiomanContextState(CancelToken());
    DiomanCancelLink? userLink;
    _pending.add(state);
    try {
      final c = call ?? const DiomanCall();
      if (c.cancelToken != null) {
        userLink = DiomanCancelLink(c.cancelToken!, state.cancel);
      }
      final options = Options(
              method: method,
              headers: c.headers,
              sendTimeout: c.sendTimeout,
              receiveTimeout: c.receiveTimeout)
          .compose(dio.options, path, queryParameters: query, data: data);
      if (c.connectTimeout != null) options.connectTimeout = c.connectTimeout;
      final ctx = DiomanContext<R>(
          id: ++_sequence,
          client: this,
          request: DiomanRequest(options, call: c),
          state: state,
          factory: response,
          keyService: DiomanKeyService(key),
          clock: clock,
          log: _log);
      var result = await run(ctx);
      if (result.kind != DiomanKind.internal) {
        result = ctx.cancelled() ?? result;
      }
      if (!result.ok && site != null && result.kind != DiomanKind.internal) {
        result = response.withCallSite(result, site);
      }
      if (result.kind == DiomanKind.internal) {
        Error.throwWithStackTrace(
            result.error ?? StateError('internal result without error'),
            result.errorStack ?? StackTrace.current);
      }
      return result;
    } finally {
      userLink?.dispose();
      state.dispose();
      _pending.remove(state);
    }
  }
}
import 'dart:async';
import 'package:dio/dio.dart';
import 'context.dart';
import 'response.dart';

/// 全部 HTTP 状态交给工厂，不让 Dio 构造 badResponse。
bool _acceptStatus(int? _) => true;

/// 递归克隆 JSON 请求体；FormData 使用 Dio 的 clone，其他类型保留身份。
Object? _clone(Object? value, DiomanLogger log) {
  if (value is Map) return value.map((k, v) => MapEntry(k, _clone(v, log)));
  if (value is List) return value.map((v) => _clone(v, log)).toList();
  if (value is FormData) return value.clone();
  if (value != null && value is! num && value is! bool && value is! String) {
    log('请求体无法克隆，回退原引用');
  }
  return value;
}

/// 执行一次发送；传输构造、Dio 及工厂异常都在本边界归一化。
Future<DiomanResponse<dynamic>>
    diomanTransport<R extends DiomanResponse<dynamic>>(
        Dio dio, DiomanContext<R> ctx) async {
  try {
    final cancelled = ctx.cancelled();
    if (cancelled != null) return cancelled;
    final options = ctx.request.toRequestOptions()
      ..validateStatus = _acceptStatus
      ..cancelToken = ctx.cancelToken;
    if (ctx.replayable) options.data = _clone(options.data, ctx.log);
    final raw = await dio.fetch<dynamic>(options);
    return ctx.factory
        .normalize(ctx.request, raw: raw, cancelled: ctx.isCancelled);
  } catch (e, s) {
    return ctx.factory
        .normalize(ctx.request, error: e, stack: s, cancelled: ctx.isCancelled);
  }
}
import 'constants.dart';
import 'dart:async';
import 'package:dioman/pipeline.dart';
import 'package:dio/dio.dart';
import 'fake_clock.dart';
import 'fake_network.dart';

/// 测试共用结果类型。
typedef Result = DiomanResponse<dynamic>;

/// 测试中间件回调。
typedef Handler = Future<Result> Function(
    DiomanContext<Result>, DiomanNext<Result>);

/// 可观察 setup 和 dispose 的插件配置。
class TestPlugin extends DiomanPlugin<Result> {
  /// 创建测试插件。
  TestPlugin(this.name,
      {this.handler,
      this.stage = DiomanStage.attempt,
      this.enforce = DiomanEnforce.post,
      this.enabled = true,
      this.onSetup,
      this.onDispose,
      this.setupOnly = false});

  /// 唯一名称。
  @override
  final String name;

  /// 挂载阶段。
  @override
  final DiomanStage stage;

  /// 阶段内位置。
  @override
  final DiomanEnforce enforce;

  /// 开关。
  @override
  final bool enabled;

  /// 中间件。
  final Handler? handler;

  /// 安装观察器。
  final void Function()? onSetup;

  /// 释放观察器。
  final void Function()? onDispose;

  /// 是否只有 setup。
  final bool setupOnly;

  /// 安装次数。
  int setups = 0;

  /// 创建隔离状态实例。
  @override
  TestInstance? setup(Dioman<Result> client) {
    setups++;
    onSetup?.call();
    return setupOnly ? null : TestInstance(handler, onDispose);
  }
}

/// 独立测试实例，counter 可用于证明 extend 无共享状态。
class TestInstance extends DiomanPluginInstance<Result> {
  /// 保存回调。
  TestInstance(this.handler, this.onDispose);

  /// 执行处理器。
  final Handler? handler;

  /// 生命周期回调。
  final void Function()? onDispose;

  /// 当前实例执行数。
  int count = 0;

  /// 执行或直通。
  @override
  Future<Result> handle(DiomanContext<Result> ctx, DiomanNext<Result> next) {
    count++;
    return handler == null ? next() : handler!(ctx, next);
  }

  /// 释放本实例。
  @override
  void dispose() => onDispose?.call();
}

/// 创建使用假网络的客户端。
Dioman<Result> testClient(
        {FakeNetwork? network,
        List<DiomanPlugin<Result>> plugins = const [],
        DiomanResponseFactory<Result>? response,
        Object? debug,
        FakeClock? clock,
        Object Function(Result)? reject,
        DiomanKeyOptions key = const DiomanKeyOptions()}) =>
    Dioman<Result>(
        dio: Dio(BaseOptions(baseUrl: pipelineTestHttpsExampleTest))
          ..httpClientAdapter = network ?? FakeNetwork(),
        plugins: plugins,
        response: response,
        debug: debug,
        reject: reject,
        key: key,
        clock: clock ?? FakeClock());

/// 创建纯单元上下文，不执行客户端发送。
DiomanContext<Result> testContext(
        {CancelToken? token,
        FakeClock? clock,
        DiomanRequest? request,
        DiomanResponseFactory<Result>? factory,
        DiomanKeyOptions key = const DiomanKeyOptions(),
        DiomanLogger? log}) =>
    DiomanContext(
        id: 1,
        client: testClient(),
        request:
            request ?? DiomanRequest(RequestOptions(path: pipelineTestUnit)),
        state: DiomanContextState(token ?? CancelToken()),
        factory: factory ?? const DiomanResponseFactory<Result>(),
        keyService: DiomanKeyService(key),
        clock: clock ?? FakeClock(),
        log: log ?? (_) {});

/// 清空微任务队列，用于等待 Dio 到达假 adapter，不推进真实时钟。
Future<void> flush() async {
  for (var i = 0; i < 30; i++) {
    await Future<void>.value();
  }
}

import 'package:dio/dio.dart';

/// 插件请求级选项键；按对象身份隔离，建议使用静态 final 实例。
class DiomanOptionKey<T> {
  /// [description] 仅供调试，例如 `DiomanOptionKey<int>('retry')`。
  DiomanOptionKey(this.description);

  /// 调试说明。
  final String description;
}

/// 解析统一选项；省略/true 复用 [base]，false 关闭；[optIn] 控制省略时关闭。
/// [merge] 合并类型化覆盖，[shorthand] 返回简写解析值；未知类型抛 ArgumentError。
/// 示例：`resolveOption<int>(true, base: 3, merge: (a, b) => b)`。
T? resolveOption<T>(Object? value,
    {required T base,
    required T Function(T base, T override) merge,
    T? Function(Object value)? shorthand,
    bool optIn = false}) {
  if (value == false || (value == null && optIn)) return null;
  if (value == null || value == true) return base;
  if (value is T) return merge(base, value as T);
  final short = shorthand?.call(value);
  if (short != null) return merge(base, short);
  throw ArgumentError.value(value, 'value', '不支持的插件选项');
}

/// 一次逻辑调用的配置；省略字段沿用 Dio 或客户端配置。
class DiomanCall {
  /// 创建调用配置，例如 `DiomanCall(key: false, envelope: false)`。
  const DiomanCall(
      {this.headers,
      this.connectTimeout,
      this.sendTimeout,
      this.receiveTimeout,
      this.cancelToken,
      this.key,
      this.envelope,
      this.silent = false,
      this.options = const {}});

  /// 按不区分大小写的键覆盖请求头。
  final Map<String, dynamic>? headers;

  /// 连接超时。
  final Duration? connectTimeout;

  /// 发送超时。
  final Duration? sendTimeout;

  /// 接收超时。
  final Duration? receiveTimeout;

  /// 用户取消令牌。
  final CancelToken? cancelToken;

  /// String、false 或 DiomanKeyOptions；null 为自动计算。
  final Object? key;

  /// false 禁用本次信封识别。
  final bool? envelope;

  /// 是否静默通知。
  final bool silent;

  /// 插件的类型化请求级选项。
  final Map<DiomanOptionKey, Object?> options;
}

/// 验证每个异步阶段的取消与窗口收尾。
void main() {
  test('[DiomanPlugin.stage] B3 固定阶段、名称和每客户端独立状态', () async {
    final retry = const DiomanRetry();
    final breaker = const DiomanBreaker();
    final auth = b3Auth(TestTokens());
    expect(retry.stage, DiomanStage.attempt);
    expect(breaker.stage, DiomanStage.attempt);
    expect(auth.stage, DiomanStage.credential);
    final a = testClient(plugins: [breaker]);
    final b = testClient(plugins: [breaker]);
    final m = a.plugin<DiomanBreakerApi>()!;
    final request = DiomanRequest(RequestOptions(path: b3Path));
    expect(m.isOpen(request), isFalse);
    expect(identical(m, b.plugin<DiomanBreakerApi>()), isFalse);
    a.dispose();
    b.dispose();
  });
  test('[DiomanRetry.next] 重发之后 internal 原样抛出，取消禁止第三次尝试', () async {
    for (final internal in [false, true]) {
      final clock = FakeClock();
      var entered = 0;
      final bug = StateError('second');
      final api = testClient(clock: clock, plugins: [
        const DiomanRetry(max: 3),
        TestPlugin('result', stage: DiomanStage.source,
            handler: (ctx, next) async {
          entered++;
          if (entered == 2) {
            ctx.state.cancel();
            if (internal) throw bug;
          }
          return ctx.fail(const DiomanFailure(kind: DiomanKind.network));
        })
      ]);
      final p = b3Finish(api.send<Object?>('GET', b3Path), clock);
      if (internal) {
        await expectLater(p, throwsA(same(bug)));
      } else {
        expect((await p).kind, DiomanKind.cancel);
      }
      expect(entered, 2);
      api.dispose();
    }
  });
  test('[DiomanAuth.next] 下游返回前取消不路由，过期通知并发去重且取消后以 cancel 结束', () async {
    var notices = 0;
    final started = Completer<void>();
    final gate = Completer<void>();
    final tm = TestTokens();
    var sent = 0;
    final api = testClient(plugins: [
      b3Auth(tm,
          expired: (t, r) async {
            notices++;
            started.complete();
            await gate.future;
          },
          failure: (r, t) => DiomanAuthFailureAction.expired),
      TestPlugin('result', stage: DiomanStage.source,
          handler: (ctx, next) async {
        sent++;
        if (sent == 1) {
          await gate.future;
          ctx.state.cancel();
        }
        return ctx
            .fail(const DiomanFailure(kind: DiomanKind.http, status: 401));
      })
    ]);
    final first = api.send<Object?>('GET', b3Path);
    await b3Turn();
    final second = api.send<Object?>('GET', b3Path);
    await started.future;
    gate.complete();
    expect((await first).kind, DiomanKind.cancel);
    expect((await second).reason, 'expired');
    expect(notices, 1);
    api.dispose();
  });
  test('[DiomanAuth.session.dedup] 同会话多个在途 expired 只清理通知一次', () async {
    final gates = List.generate(2, (_) => Completer<void>());
    var sent = 0;
    var notices = 0;
    final tm = TestTokens();
    final api = testClient(plugins: [
      b3Auth(tm,
          failure: (r, t) => DiomanAuthFailureAction.expired,
          expired: (t, r) {
            notices++;
          }),
      TestPlugin('result', stage: DiomanStage.source,
          handler: (ctx, next) async {
        final index = sent++;
        await gates[index].future;
        return ctx
            .fail(const DiomanFailure(kind: DiomanKind.http, status: 401));
      })
    ]);
    final a = api.send<Object?>('GET', b3Path);
    final b = api.send<Object?>('GET', b3Path);
    await b3Turn();
    gates[0].complete();
    await a;
    gates[1].complete();
    await b;
    expect(tm.clears, 1);
    expect(notices, 1);
    api.dispose();
  });
  test('[DiomanAuth.proactive.cancel] 主动刷新等待取消也不终止共享刷新', () async {
    final tm = TestTokens();
    final gate = Completer<void>();
    final started = Completer<void>();
    final api = testClient(plugins: [
      b3Auth(tm,
          expiresAt: (t) => DateTime.fromMillisecondsSinceEpoch(0),
          refresh: (t, r) async {
            started.complete();
            await gate.future;
            t.setTokens(accessToken: b3Fresh);
          })
    ]);
    final p = api.send<Object?>('GET', b3Path);
    await started.future;
    api.cancelAll();
    expect((await p).kind, DiomanKind.cancel);
    gate.complete();
    await b3Turn();
    expect(tm.accessToken, b3Fresh);
    api.dispose();
  });
}
  Future<bool>? _refreshing;

  /// 已通知过期的会话凭证。
  String? _expiredSession;

  /// 稳定的失败原因。
  static const _expired = 'expired';

  /// 当前存储。
  DiomanTokenManager get _tokens => config.tokenManager;

  /// 当前是否仍有本地凭证。
  bool get _hasToken => _tokens.accessToken?.isNotEmpty == true;

  /// 通知异常隔离，不影响刷新或请求结果。
  Future<void> _notice(DiomanContext<R> ctx, DiomanAuthNotice notice,
      DiomanResponse<dynamic> r) async {
    try {
      await notice(_tokens, r);
    } catch (e, s) {
      ctx.client.logError('[${DiomanAuth.pluginName}] 鉴权通知失败: $e\n$s');
    }
  }

  /// 只结束失败请求所属会话，旧请求不能清新凭证。
  Future<void> _expire(DiomanContext<R> ctx, DiomanAuthOptions o,
      DiomanResponse<dynamic> r, String? session) async {
    final current = _tokens.accessToken;
    if (_hasToken && current != session) return;
    if (!_hasToken && session == _expiredSession) return;
    _expiredSession = session;
    _tokens.clear();
    await _notice(ctx, o.onAccessExpired!, r);
  }

  /// 启动强制异步刷新；窗口发布先于用户回调，同步抛错也必释放。
  Future<bool> _refresh(DiomanContext<R> ctx, DiomanAuthOptions o,
      DiomanResponse<dynamic> r, String? session) {
    return _refreshing ??= (() async {
      try {
        await Future<void>(() => config.onRefresh(_tokens, r));
        return true;
      } catch (e, s) {
        ctx.log('[${DiomanAuth.pluginName}] 刷新失败: $e\n$s');
        await _expire(ctx, o, r, session);
        return false;
      } finally {
        _refreshing = null;
      }
    })();
  }

  /// 失败载体统一由工厂创建。
  DiomanResponse<dynamic> _fail(DiomanContext<R> ctx, String reason) =>
      ctx.fail(DiomanFailure(kind: DiomanKind.auth, reason: reason));

  /// 加入刷新后立刻重查本调用的取消，不中止共享刷新。
  Future<DiomanResponse<dynamic>?> _join(
      DiomanContext<R> ctx, Future<bool> pending) async {
    final success = await ctx.wait(pending);
    final cancelled = ctx.cancelled();
    return cancelled ?? (success == true ? null : _fail(ctx, _expired));
  }

  /// 每次尝试入口求值保护规则，同一尝试内至多重放一次。
  @override
  Future<DiomanResponse<dynamic>> handle(
      DiomanContext<R> ctx, DiomanNext<R> next) async {
    final o = resolveOption<DiomanAuthOptions>(
        ctx.request.call.options[DiomanAuth.option],
        base: base,
        merge: _merge);
    if (o == null ||
        o.enabled == false ||
        o.isProtected?.call(ctx.request) == false) {
      return next();
    }
    ctx.markReplayable();
    final pending = _refreshing;
    if (pending != null) {
      final failure = await _join(ctx, pending);
      final cancelled = ctx.cancelled();
      if (cancelled != null) return cancelled;
      if (failure != null) return failure;
    }
    var replayed = false;
    var proactive = false;
    while (true) {
      // 重放前必须重查凭证，刷新成功也可能已被使用者登出。
      if (!_hasToken) {
        final r = _fail(ctx, 'unauthenticated');
        await _notice(ctx, o.onAccessDenied ?? o.onAccessExpired!, r);
        return ctx.cancelled() ?? r;
      }
      // 登录（包括拿到同名凭证）之后允许新一轮过期通知。
      _expiredSession = null;
      final at = o.expiresAt?.call(_tokens.accessToken!);
      if (!proactive &&
          at != null &&
          !at.isAfter(ctx.clock.now().add(o.refreshLeeway!))) {
        proactive = true;
        final failure = await _join(
            ctx, _refresh(ctx, o, _fail(ctx, _expired), _tokens.accessToken));
        final cancelled = ctx.cancelled();
        if (cancelled != null) return cancelled;
        if (failure != null) return failure;
        continue;
      }
      RequestPatch? patch;
      String? token;
      for (var i = 0; i < DiomanAuth.readyStableAttempts; i++) {
        token = _tokens.accessToken;
        patch = o.ready == null
            ? RequestPatch(headers: {o.headerKey!: o.buildHeader!(token!)})
            : await o.ready!(_tokens, ctx.request);
        final cancelled = ctx.cancelled();
        if (cancelled != null) return cancelled;
        final current = _tokens.accessToken;
        if (current == token) break;
        // 最后一次仍不稳定就不发送不明凭证，留待使用者修正 ready。
        if (i == DiomanAuth.readyStableAttempts - 1) {
          throw StateError('ready 期间凭证持续变化');
        }
        if (!_hasToken) break;
      }
      if (!_hasToken) continue;
      ctx.set(DiomanAuth.injectedToken, token);
      final r = await next(patch);
      final cancelled = ctx.cancelled();
      if (r.kind == DiomanKind.internal) return r;
      if (cancelled != null) return cancelled;
      if (r.ok || r.kind == DiomanKind.cancel) return r;
      final action = o.onFailure == null
          ? defaultAuthFailure(r, _tokens, ctx.get<String?>(DiomanAuth.injectedToken))
          : await o.onFailure!(r, _tokens);
      final cancelledAfterFailure = ctx.cancelled();
      if (cancelledAfterFailure != null) return cancelledAfterFailure;
      switch (action) {
        case DiomanAuthFailureAction.refresh:
        case DiomanAuthFailureAction.replay:
          if (replayed) {
            await _expire(ctx, o, r, token);
            return ctx.cancelled() ?? _fail(ctx, _expired);
          }
          if (action == DiomanAuthFailureAction.refresh) {
            final failure = await _join(ctx, _refresh(ctx, o, r, token));
            final cancelled = ctx.cancelled();
            if (cancelled != null) return cancelled;
            if (failure != null) return failure;
          }
          replayed = true;
        case DiomanAuthFailureAction.expired:
          await _expire(ctx, o, r, token);
          return ctx.cancelled() ?? _fail(ctx, _expired);
        case DiomanAuthFailureAction.deny:
          final denied = _fail(ctx, 'denied');
          await _notice(ctx, o.onAccessDenied ?? o.onAccessExpired!, denied);
          return ctx.cancelled() ?? denied;
        case DiomanAuthFailureAction.others:
          return r;
      }
    }
  }
}

codex
Auth callback waits can prevent cancellation and request cleanup indefinitely. Static analysis and the 39 targeted B3 tests passed, but those tests do not verify cancellation before pending callbacks are released.

Review comment:

- [P2] Make asynchronous auth callbacks cancellation-aware — E:\workspaces\dart-labs-pipeline\dioman\lib\src\pipeline\plugins\auth.dart:338-342
  When an asynchronous `ready` callback remains pending, cancelling the request, calling `cancelAll()`, or disposing the client cannot complete the request: this direct await prevents execution from reaching the cancellation check. The same issue affects `onFailure` and asynchronous notices, leaving the request registered and outer loading cleanup blocked until the callback finishes. Use cancellation-aware waits for request-owned callbacks, while keeping shared refresh work independent of caller cancellation.
